\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Acceptance read-out, the AR analogue of AE's
  \isa{ae{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}simulates{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}accept{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}iff}.  Under \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}simulates}, the
  source \isa{M}-config is in the accept state iff the paired
  \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-config sits in the canonical accept config
  \isa{{\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}t{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M{\isacharcomma}{\kern0pt}\ ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}accept{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}stage\ bl{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}M{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}.  Forward: \isa{q\isactrlsub M\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ t}
  forces the second \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}simulates} disjunct (the first needs
  \isa{q\isactrlsub M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}\ {\isasymnotin}\ {\isasymlbrace}t{\isacharcomma}{\kern0pt}\ r{\isasymrbrace}}; the third needs \isa{t\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ r}, ruled
  out by \isa{valid{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}mttm{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}t{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}neq{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}r}).  Reverse is immediate from the
  state-equality conjunct.
  Throughout, \isa{b} abbreviates \isa{block{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}width\ {\isasymGamma}} (the per-symbol cell width).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The next substep, non-terminal hand-off: a single
  \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-step from an \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimNext} stage whose \isa{M}-state
  \isa{q} is neither accepting nor rejecting routes to the next
  \isa{M}-step's \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimRead} boundary, resetting both the
  current-tape and bit-counter fields to \isa{{\isadigit{0}}} while preserving
  \isa{buf}, \isa{dvec}, and the per-tape \isa{posk}.  No tape
  cell changes and no head moves (all directions \isa{N},
  the write vector is the read vector).  The cleanest of the five
  phases — the \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}compute{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} shape with arm 1 of the
  three-arm \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}delta{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}next} union; the two \isa{{\isasymdelta}LE}
  filters discharge reflexively (write equals read, move \isa{N})
  and target-stage validity rests only on \isa{{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ b}.
  The terminal arms (\isa{q\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ t{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M} / \isa{q\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ r{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M}) route
  to the halt stages instead and are handled at assembly, not here.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The two terminal hand-offs, the accept and reject arms of
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}delta{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}next}: a single \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-step from an
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimNext} stage whose \isa{M}-state \isa{q} is the
  accepting (resp. rejecting) state lands on the canonical halt stage
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}accept{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}stage\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}bl{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} (resp. \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}reject{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}stage}),
  so the full target state equals \isa{t{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}alphabet{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}reduce\ M{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}
  (resp. \isa{r{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm}): full-state
  acceptance forces the canonicalisation.  Tape and heads are
  unchanged (the move vector is all-\isa{N}, no writes).  Modelled on
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}next{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}; the membership lets \isa{blast} select the halt
  comprehension and instantiate its witnesses.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
\isakeywordONE{lemma}\isamarkupfalse%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Two position-bookkeeping facts for the advance phase, decided
  once in a clean context (no \isa{let}-bound \isa{{\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}}-vars to
  tangle the case split).  Given \isa{M}'s head at position
  \isa{p} moving \isa{dr} (with \isa{dr\ {\isasymnoteq}\ L} when
  \isa{p\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{0}}}, the \isa{{\isasymdelta}LE} discipline), the read-phase
  position-kind \isa{if\ p{\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{0}}\ then\ AtLE\ else\ if\ p{\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{1}}\ then\ FirstProper\ else\ FurtherProper} advances via \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}newpos} to \isa{AtLE}
  exactly when \isa{M}'s new position is \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}, and the
  read-phase landing position less the \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}disp} displacement is
  exactly \isa{sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos} of \isa{M}'s new position.  Both reduce to

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Per-\isa{M}-step forward simulation: one \isa{M}-step
  \isa{cM\ {\isasymrightarrow}\ cM\isactrlsub {\isadigit{1}}} from an \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimRead} boundary is matched by a
  bounded \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-phase (read \isa{{\isasymrightarrow}} compute \isa{{\isasymrightarrow}} write
  \isa{{\isasymrightarrow}} advance \isa{{\isasymrightarrow}} next) of at most \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isasymcdot}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{5}}b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}} \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-steps (the per-phase \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M}-scaled
  sum: read \isa{{\isasymle}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isasymcdot}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b{\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{2}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}, write and advance each
  \isa{{\isasymle}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isasymcdot}\ {\isadigit{2}}b}, compute and next one step apiece) that
  re-establishes \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}simulates} at the next boundary.  This is the
  composition of the per-substep phase lemmas above into one
  \isa{M}-step; the chunked engine below iterates it.  The
  reachability hypothesis \isa{reach{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}M} with \isa{w{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}sub} supplies
  the substrate \isa{LE}-only-at-position-\isa{{\isadigit{0}}} fact the read
  look-back needs, and \isa{card{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}ge} gives \isa{b\ {\isasymge}\ {\isadigit{2}}}
  for the proper-region read arms.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The post-write tape correspondence, factored out of
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}simulates{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}forward{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} so the reverse cycle-close reuses
  it.  Given the
  read-boundary correspondence between \isa{M}'s tape and the output
  tape \isa{rt}, and that \isa{cM{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{1}}} is \isa{M} after writing
  \isa{a{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}} under each head, the write phase's per-tape overwrite
  \isa{wt} — which stamps \isa{a{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}'s cell block at the head's
  block and leaves the rest of \isa{rt} untouched — is again a
  correspondence, now for \isa{cM{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{1}}}.  Both arms supply their own
  \isa{wt} and discharge \isa{wt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}def} from their write-phase
  output contract.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\isamarkupcmt{Cut-tolerant write dispatch.  The write phase keys the

     the boundary).  \isa{poskle} is that dispatch fact, read off
     \isa{{\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}rposk} with no \isa{le{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unique}.  The correspondence rebuild
     needs only the \isa{mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ cM\ k\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{0}}\ {\isasymRightarrow}\ a{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}\ k\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ le}
     direction (\isa{a{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}le{\isadigit{0}}}, by clause-1 \isa{{\isasymdelta}LE}, again no
     \isa{le{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unique}).%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Chunked-induction engine for the simulation phase, the
  AR analogue of AE's \isa{ae{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}simulation{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}phase{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}chunked}.  Given an
  accepting \isa{M}-path of length \isa{n} from a reachable \isa{cM}
  with a paired \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimRead} \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-config \isa{c{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}} satisfying
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}simulates}, exhibit a corresponding accepting
  \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-path of length at most \isa{{\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{5}}b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ {\isasymcdot}\ n} ending in the
  canonical accept config.  Unlike AE — which groups \isa{c} source
  steps per stage and needs the strong \isa{less{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}induct} with
  \isa{min\ n\ c} chunk arithmetic — AR simulates one \isa{M}-step per
  phase, so ordinary \isa{induction\ n} with the fixed per-step
  bound suffices: each step contributes \isa{{\isasymle}\ {\isadigit{5}}b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}}, summing to
  \isa{{\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{5}}b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ {\isasymcdot}\ n} by a single \isa{linarith}.  All substrate
  reasoning is delegated to \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}simulates{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}forward{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}; the
  engine only splits the trace, recurses on the tail, and composes
  the two \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-paths via \isa{relpow{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}add}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
